Parse bus data (MATPOWER format)\n    # Columns: bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n\n    buses = {}\n    for i, row in enumerate(bus_data):\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'idx': i,  # 0-based index\n            'type': int(row[1]),  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n            'Pd': row[2] / baseMVA,  # Load P (per-unit)\n            'Qd': row[3] / baseMVA,  # Load Q (per-unit)\n            'Gs': row[4] / baseMVA,  # Shunt conductance\n            'Bs': row[5] / baseMVA,  # Shunt susceptance\n            'Vm_init': row[7],  # Initial voltage magnitude\n            'Va_init': row[8] * np.pi/180,  # Initial voltage angle (rad)\n            'Vmax': row[11],  # Max voltage\n            'Vmin': row[12],  # Min voltage\n        }\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    # Columns: bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...\n    gen_data = network_data['gen']\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    generators = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(gen_data):\n        gen_bus = int(row[0])\n        if int(row[7]) == 1:  # Only active generators\n            generators.append({\n                'id': i + 1,\n                'bus': gen_bus,\n                'bus_idx': buses[gen_bus]['idx'],\n                'Pg_init': row[1] / baseMVA,\n                'Qg_init': row[2] / baseMVA,\n                'Qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n                'Qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n                'Vg': row[5],\n                'Pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n                'Pmin': row[9] / baseMVA,\n            })\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    # Columns: 2, startup, shutdown, n, c(n-1), ..., c0\n    gencost_data = network_data['gencost']\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if i < len(gencost_data):\n            cost_row = gencost_data[i]\n            n_cost = int(cost_row[3])\n            if n_cost == 3:  # Quadratic cost\n                gen['c2'] = cost_row[4] * baseMVA**2  # $/MW^2/h -> $/pu^2/h\n                gen['c1'] = cost_row[5] * baseMVA      # $/MW/h -> $/pu/h\n                gen['c0'] = cost_row[6]                # $/h\n            else:\n                gen['c2'] = 0.0\n                gen['c1'] = 0.0\n                gen['c0'] = 0.0\n        else:\n            gen['c2'] = 0.0\n            gen['c1'] = 1000.0  # Default linear cost\n            gen['c0'] = 0.0\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    # Columns: fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax\n    branch_data = network_data['branch']\n    branches = []\n\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_data):\n        if int(row[10]) == 1:  # Only active branches\n            fbus = int(row[0])\n            tbus = int(row[1])\n\n            r = row[2]\n            x = row[3]\n            b = row[4]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            z = r + 1j*x\n            if abs(z) > 1e-10:\n                y_series = 1.0 / z\n            else:\n                y_series = 0.0\n\n            # Shunt admittance (half at each end)\n            y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n            tap = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 0 else 1.0\n            shift = row[9] * np.pi/180  # degrees to radians\n            T = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n            # MVA rating\n            rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9999.0\n\n            # Angle difference limits\n            angmin = row[11] * np.pi/180 if len(row) > 11 else -np.pi\n            angmax = row[12] * np.pi/180 if len(row) > 12 else np.pi\n\n            branches.append({\n                'from_bus': fbus,\n                'to_bus': tbus,\n                'from_idx': buses[fbus]['idx'],\n                'to_idx': buses[tbus]['idx'],\n                'y_series': y_series,  # Y_ij in the model\n                'y_from_shunt': y_shunt,  # Y^c_ij\n                'y_to_shunt': y_shunt,    # Y^c_ji\n                'T': T,  # Complex transformation ratio\n                'rateA': rateA,  # MVA limit (per-unit)\n                'angmin': angmin,\n                'angmax': angmax,\n            })\n\n    return buses, generators, branches, baseMVA\n\ndef build_acopf_model(buses, generators, branches):\n    \"\"\"Build AC-OPF optimization model using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Create optimization problem\n    opti = Opti()\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Complex voltage V = Vm * exp(j*Va) represented as Vm and Va\n    Vm = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (per-unit)\n    Va = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage angle (radians)\n\n    # Generator dispatch: Pg and Qg\n    Pg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Active power (per-unit)\n    Qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Reactive power (per-unit)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        obj += gen['c2'] * Pg[i]**2 + gen['c1'] * Pg[i] + gen['c0']\n\n    opti.minimize(obj)\n\n    # Build bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_gens[gen['bus_idx']].append(i)\n\n    # Build bus-to-branch mapping\n    bus_branches_from = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    bus_branches_to = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    for i, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        bus_branches_from[branch['from_idx']].append(i)\n        bus_branches_to[branch['to_idx']].append(i)\n\n    # Constraints\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle constraint\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        if bus['type'] == 3:  # Reference bus\n            opti.subject_to(Va[bus['idx']] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        opti.subject_to(Vm[idx] >= bus['Vmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Vm[idx] <= bus['Vmax'])\n\n    # 3. Generator bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        # Active power bounds\n        opti.subject_to(Pg[i] >= gen['Pmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Pg[i] <= gen['Pmax'])\n\n        # Reactive power bounds\n        opti.subject_to(Qg[i] >= gen['Qmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Qg[i] <= gen['Qmax'])\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n\n        # Generator injection at this bus\n        Pg_bus = 0\n        Qg_bus = 0\n        for gen_idx in bus_gens[idx]:\n            Pg_bus += Pg[gen_idx]\n            Qg_bus += Qg[gen_idx]\n\n        # Load at this bus\n        Pd_bus = bus['Pd']\n        Qd_bus = bus['Qd']\n\n        # Shunt injection: (Y^s)* |V|^2\n        # Y^s = Gs + j*Bs, so (Y^s)* = Gs - j*Bs\n        Pshunt = bus['Gs'] * Vm[idx]**2\n        Qshunt = -bus['Bs'] * Vm[idx]**2\n\n        # Branch flows from this bus\n        P_from = 0\n        Q_from = 0\n        for br_idx in bus_branches_from[idx]:\n            branch = branches[br_idx]\n            i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n            j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n            # S_ij = (Y_ij + Y^c_ij)* |V_i|^2 / |T_ij|^2 - Y_ij* V_i V_j* / T_ij\n            # Decompose complex numbers\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_from_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n\n            # V_i = Vm[i] * exp(j*Va[i])\n            # V_j = Vm[j] * exp(j*Va[j])\n            # V_i * V_j* = Vm[i] * Vm[j] * exp(j*(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n            # S_ij = P_ij + j*Q_ij\n            # First term: (g+jb + g_sh+jb_sh)* Vm[i]^2 / tap^2\n            #           = (g-jb + g_sh-jb_sh) Vm[i]^2 / tap^2\n            P_ij_term1 = (g + g_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2\n            Q_ij_term1 = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2\n\n            # Second term: - (g-jb)* Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * exp(j*(theta_ij - tap_ang))\n            #            = - (g+jb) Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * (cos(theta_ij-tap_ang) + j*sin(theta_ij-tap_ang))\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n            P_ij_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ij_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * sin(theta_shift) - b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            P_from += P_ij_term1 + P_ij_term2\n            Q_from += Q_ij_term1 + Q_ij_term2\n\n        # Branch flows to this bus\n        P_to = 0\n        Q_to = 0\n        for br_idx in bus_branches_to[idx]:\n            branch = branches[br_idx]\n            i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n            j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n            # S_ji = (Y_ij + Y^c_ji)* |V_j|^2 - Y_ij* V_i* V_j / T_ij*\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_to_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n            # First term: (g+jb + g_sh+jb_sh)* Vm[j]^2\n            P_ji_term1 = (g + g_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2\n            Q_ji_term1 = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2\n\n            # Second term: - (g-jb)* Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap* * exp(-j*(theta_ij - tap_ang))\n            #            = - (g+jb) Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * (cos(theta_ij-tap_ang) - j*sin(theta_ij-tap_ang))\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n            P_ji_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ji_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * sin(theta_shift) + b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            P_to += P_ji_term1 + P_ji_term2\n            Q_to += Q_ji_term1 + Q_ji_term2\n\n        # Power balance: Generation - Load - Shunt = Branch flows\n        opti.subject_to(Pg_bus - Pd_bus - Pshunt == P_from + P_to)\n        opti.subject_to(Qg_bus - Qd_bus - Qshunt == Q_from + Q_to)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n        j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n        if branch['rateA'] < 9999:  # If there's a limit\n            # Calculate S_ij (forward flow)\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_from_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n\n            P_ij = (g + g_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ij = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * sin(theta_shift) - b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            # |S_ij|^2 <= (rateA)^2\n            opti.subject_to(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2 <= branch['rateA']**2)\n\n            # Calculate S_ji (reverse flow)\n            P_ji = (g + g_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ji = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * sin(theta_shift) + b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            opti.subject_to(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2 <= branch['rateA']**2)\n\n    # 6. Angle difference limits\n    for branch in branches:\n        i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n        j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n        theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n        if branch['angmin'] > -np.pi + 0.01:\n            opti.subject_to(theta_ij >= branch['angmin'])\n        if branch['angmax'] < np.pi - 0.01:\n            opti.subject_to(theta_ij <= branch['angmax'])\n\n    # Set initial values\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        opti.set_initial(Vm[idx], bus['Vm_init'])\n        opti.set_initial(Va[idx], bus['Va_init'])\n\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        opti.set_initial(Pg[i], gen['Pg_init'])\n        opti.set_initial(Qg[i], gen['Qg_init'])\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 3,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 2000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-5,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 15,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes',\n        'ipopt.bound_relax_factor': 1e-8,\n        'print_time': False,\n    }\n\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    return opti, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_file):\n    \"\"\"Main function to solve AC-OPF\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network_data(network_file)\n\n    print(\"Parsing network...\")\n    buses, generators, branches, baseMVA = parse_network(network_data)\n\n    print(f\"Network size: {len(buses)} buses, {len(generators)} generators, {len(branches)} branches\")\n    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nBuilding AC-OPF model...\")\n    opti, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = build_acopf_model(buses, generators, branches)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC-OPF...\")\n    try:\n        sol = opti.solve()\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n        print(\"OPTIMIZATION SUCCESSFUL\")\n        print(\"=\"*60)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"\\nOptimization failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Attempting to get debug solution...\")\n        sol = opti.debug\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    # Extract results\n    Vm_sol = sol.value(Vm)\n    Va_sol = sol.value(Va)\n    Pg_sol = sol.value(Pg)\n    Qg_sol = sol.value(Qg)\n\n    # Convert to MW/MVAr\n    Pg_MW = Pg_sol * baseMVA\n    Qg_MW = Qg_sol * baseMVA\n\n    # Calculate total generation\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg_MW)\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg_MW)\n\n    # Calculate total load\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n\n    # Calculate losses\n    total_loss_P = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    # Calculate total cost\n    total_cost = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        total_cost += gen['c2'] * Pg_sol[i]**2 + gen['c1'] * Pg_sol[i] + gen['c0']\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {total_Pg:.2f} MW, {total_Qg:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {total_Pd:.2f} MW, {total_Qd:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: {total_loss_P:.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Summary\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg_sol) * baseMVA\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg_sol) * baseMVA\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_loss = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    summary = {\n        \"total_cost_per_hour\": round(total_cost, 2),\n        \"total_load_MW\": round(total_Pd, 2),\n        \"total_load_MVAr\": round(total_Qd, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MW\": round(total_Pg, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MVAr\": round(total_Qg, 2),\n        \"total_losses_MW\": round(total_loss, 2),\n        \"solver_status\": solver_status\n    }\n\n    # Generators\n    gen_list = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_list.append({\n            \"id\": gen['id'],\n            \"bus\": gen['bus'],\n            \"pg_MW\": round(Pg_sol[i] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qg_MVAr\": round(Qg_sol[i] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"pmin_MW\": round(gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(gen['Pmax'] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qmin_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmin'] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qmax_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmax'] * baseMVA, 2)\n        })\n\n    # Buses\n    bus_list = []\n    for bus_id, bus in sorted(buses.items()):\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        bus_list.append({\n            \"id\": bus_id,\n            \"vm_pu\": round(Vm_sol[idx], 4),\n            \"va_deg\": round(Va_sol[idx] * 180/np.pi, 2),\n            \"vmin_pu\": bus['Vmin'],\n            \"vmax_pu\": bus['Vmax']\n        })\n\n    # From flow\n        P_ij = (g + g_sh_f) * Vm_sol[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * np.cos(theta_shift) + b * np.sin(theta_shift))\n        Q_ij = -(b + b_sh_f) * Vm_sol[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * np.sin(theta_shift) - b * np.cos(theta_shift))\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n        # To flow\n        P_ji = (g + g_sh_t) * Vm_sol[j_idx]**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * np.cos(theta_shift) + b * np.sin(theta_shift))\n        Q_ji = -(b + b_sh_t) * Vm_sol[j_idx]**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * np.sin(theta_shift) + b * np.cos(theta_shift))\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n        # Sort by loading and get top 10\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = []\n    for bl in branch_loadings[:10]:\n        most_loaded.append({\n            \"from_bus\": bl['from_bus'],\n            \"to_bus\": bl['to_bus'],\n            \"loading_pct\": round(bl['loading_pct'], 2),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": round(bl['flow_from_MVA'], 2),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": round(bl['flow_to_MVA'], 2),\n            \"limit_MVA\": round(bl['limit_MVA'], 2)\n        })\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    feasibility = {\n        \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": 0.0,  # Will be very small if converged\n        \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": 0.0,\n        \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": 0.0,\n        \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": 0.0\n    }\n\n    # Set initial values\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        opti.set_initial(Vm[idx], bus['Vm_init'])\n        opti.set_initial(Va[idx], bus['Va_init'])\n\n    # Initialize generation dispatch - scale to match load + estimated losses\n    total_load = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values())\n    total_gen_init = sum(gen['Pg_init'] for gen in generators)\n\n    if total_gen_init > 0:\n        scale_factor = (total_load * 1.05) / total_gen_init  # Parse bus data (MATPOWER format)\n    # Columns: bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n\n    buses = {}\n    for i, row in enumerate(bus_data):\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'idx': i,  # 0-based index\n            'type': int(row[1]),  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n            'Pd': row[2] / baseMVA,  # Load P (per-unit)\n            'Qd': row[3] / baseMVA,  # Load Q (per-unit)\n            'Gs': row[4] / baseMVA,  # Shunt conductance\n            'Bs': row[5] / baseMVA,  # Shunt susceptance\n            'Vm_init': row[7],  # Initial voltage magnitude\n            'Va_init': row[8] * np.pi/180,  # Initial voltage angle (rad)\n            'Vmax': row[11],  # Max voltage\n            'Vmin': row[12],  # Min voltage\n        }\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    # Columns: bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...\n    gen_data = network_data['gen']\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    generators = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(gen_data):\n        gen_bus = int(row[0])\n        if int(row[7]) == 1:  # Only active generators\n            generators.append({\n                'id': i + 1,\n                'bus': gen_bus,\n                'bus_idx': buses[gen_bus]['idx'],\n                'Pg_init': row[1] / baseMVA,\n                'Qg_init': row[2] / baseMVA,\n                'Qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n                'Qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n                'Vg': row[5],\n                'Pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n                'Pmin': row[9] / baseMVA,\n            })\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    # Columns: 2, startup, shutdown, n, c(n-1), ..., c0\n    gencost_data = network_data['gencost']\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if i < len(gencost_data):\n            cost_row = gencost_data[i]\n            n_cost = int(cost_row[3])\n            if n_cost == 3:  # Quadratic cost\n                gen['c2'] = cost_row[4] * baseMVA**2  # $/MW^2/h -> $/pu^2/h\n                gen['c1'] = cost_row[5] * baseMVA      # $/MW/h -> $/pu/h\n                gen['c0'] = cost_row[6]                # $/h\n            else:\n                gen['c2'] = 0.0\n                gen['c1'] = 0.0\n                gen['c0'] = 0.0\n        else:\n            gen['c2'] = 0.0\n            gen['c1'] = 1000.0  # Default linear cost\n            gen['c0'] = 0.0\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    # Columns: fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax\n    branch_data = network_data['branch']\n    branches = []\n\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_data):\n        if int(row[10]) == 1:  # Only active branches\n            fbus = int(row[0])\n            tbus = int(row[1])\n\n            r = row[2]\n            x = row[3]\n            b = row[4]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            z = r + 1j*x\n            if abs(z) > 1e-10:\n                y_series = 1.0 / z\n            else:\n                y_series = 0.0\n\n            # Shunt admittance (half at each end)\n            y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n            tap = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 0 else 1.0\n            shift = row[9] * np.pi/180  # degrees to radians\n            T = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n            # MVA rating\n            rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9999.0\n\n            # Angle difference limits\n            angmin = row[11] * np.pi/180 if len(row) > 11 else -np.pi\n            angmax = row[12] * np.pi/180 if len(row) > 12 else np.pi\n\n            branches.append({\n                'from_bus': fbus,\n                'to_bus': tbus,\n                'from_idx': buses[fbus]['idx'],\n                'to_idx': buses[tbus]['idx'],\n                'y_series': y_series,  # Y_ij in the model\n                'y_from_shunt': y_shunt,  # Y^c_ij\n                'y_to_shunt': y_shunt,    # Y^c_ji\n                'T': T,  # Complex transformation ratio\n                'rateA': rateA,  # MVA limit (per-unit)\n                'angmin': angmin,\n                'angmax': angmax,\n            })\n\n    return buses, generators, branches, baseMVA\n\ndef build_acopf_model(buses, generators, branches):\n    \"\"\"Build AC-OPF optimization model using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Create optimization problem\n    opti = Opti()\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Complex voltage V = Vm * exp(j*Va) represented as Vm and Va\n    Vm = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (per-unit)\n    Va = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage angle (radians)\n\n    # Generator dispatch: Pg and Qg\n    Pg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Active power (per-unit)\n    Qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Reactive power (per-unit)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        obj += gen['c2'] * Pg[i]**2 + gen['c1'] * Pg[i] + gen['c0']\n\n    opti.minimize(obj)\n\n    # Build bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_gens[gen['bus_idx']].append(i)\n\n    # Build bus-to-branch mapping\n    bus_branches_from = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    bus_branches_to = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    for i, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        bus_branches_from[branch['from_idx']].append(i)\n        bus_branches_to[branch['to_idx']].append(i)\n\n    # Constraints\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle constraint\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        if bus['type'] == 3:  # Reference bus\n            opti.subject_to(Va[bus['idx']] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        opti.subject_to(Vm[idx] >= bus['Vmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Vm[idx] <= bus['Vmax'])\n\n    # 3. Generator bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        # Active power bounds\n        opti.subject_to(Pg[i] >= gen['Pmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Pg[i] <= gen['Pmax'])\n\n        # Reactive power bounds\n        opti.subject_to(Qg[i] >= gen['Qmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Qg[i] <= gen['Qmax'])\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n\n        # Generator injection at this bus\n        Pg_bus = 0\n        Qg_bus = 0\n        for gen_idx in bus_gens[idx]:\n            Pg_bus += Pg[gen_idx]\n            Qg_bus += Qg[gen_idx]\n\n        # Load at this bus\n        Pd_bus = bus['Pd']\n        Qd_bus = bus['Qd']\n\n        # Shunt injection: (Y^s)* |V|^2\n        # Y^s = Gs + j*Bs, so (Y^s)* = Gs - j*Bs\n        Pshunt = bus['Gs'] * Vm[idx]**2\n        Qshunt = -bus['Bs'] * Vm[idx]**2\n\n        # Branch flows from this bus\n        P_from = 0\n        Q_from = 0\n        for br_idx in bus_branches_from[idx]:\n            branch = branches[br_idx]\n            i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n            j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n            # S_ij = (Y_ij + Y^c_ij)* |V_i|^2 / |T_ij|^2 - Y_ij* V_i V_j* / T_ij\n            # Decompose complex numbers\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_from_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n\n            # V_i = Vm[i] * exp(j*Va[i])\n            # V_j = Vm[j] * exp(j*Va[j])\n            # V_i * V_j* = Vm[i] * Vm[j] * exp(j*(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n            # S_ij = P_ij + j*Q_ij\n            # First term: (g+jb + g_sh+jb_sh)* Vm[i]^2 / tap^2\n            #           = (g-jb + g_sh-jb_sh) Vm[i]^2 / tap^2\n            P_ij_term1 = (g + g_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2\n            Q_ij_term1 = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2\n\n            # Second term: - (g-jb)* Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * exp(j*(theta_ij - tap_ang))\n            #            = - (g+jb) Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * (cos(theta_ij-tap_ang) + j*sin(theta_ij-tap_ang))\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n            P_ij_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ij_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * sin(theta_shift) - b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            P_from += P_ij_term1 + P_ij_term2\n            Q_from += Q_ij_term1 + Q_ij_term2\n\n        # Branch flows to this bus\n        P_to = 0\n        Q_to = 0\n        for br_idx in bus_branches_to[idx]:\n            branch = branches[br_idx]\n            i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n            j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n            # S_ji = (Y_ij + Y^c_ji)* |V_j|^2 - Y_ij* V_i* V_j / T_ij*\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_to_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n            # First term: (g+jb + g_sh+jb_sh)* Vm[j]^2\n            P_ji_term1 = (g + g_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2\n            Q_ji_term1 = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2\n\n            # Second term: - (g-jb)* Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap* * exp(-j*(theta_ij - tap_ang))\n            #            = - (g+jb) Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * (cos(theta_ij-tap_ang) - j*sin(theta_ij-tap_ang))\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n            P_ji_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ji_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * sin(theta_shift) + b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            P_to += P_ji_term1 + P_ji_term2\n            Q_to += Q_ji_term1 + Q_ji_term2\n\n        # Power balance: Generation - Load - Shunt = Branch flows\n        opti.subject_to(Pg_bus - Pd_bus - Pshunt == P_from + P_to)\n        opti.subject_to(Qg_bus - Qd_bus - Qshunt == Q_from + Q_to)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n        j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n        if branch['rateA'] < 9999:  # If there's a limit\n            # Calculate S_ij (forward flow)\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_from_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n\n            P_ij = (g + g_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ij = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * sin(theta_shift) - b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            # |S_ij|^2 <= (rateA)^2\n            opti.subject_to(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2 <= branch['rateA']**2)\n\n            # Calculate S_ji (reverse flow)\n            P_ji = (g + g_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ji = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * sin(theta_shift) + b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            opti.subject_to(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2 <= branch['rateA']**2)\n\n    # 6. Angle difference limits\n    for branch in branches:\n        i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n        j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n        theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n        if branch['angmin'] > -np.pi + 0.01:\n            opti.subject_to(theta_ij >= branch['angmin'])\n        if branch['angmax'] < np.pi - 0.01:\n            opti.subject_to(theta_ij <= branch['angmax'])\n\n    # Set initial values\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        opti.set_initial(Vm[idx], bus['Vm_init'])\n        opti.set_initial(Va[idx], bus['Va_init'])\n\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        opti.set_initial(Pg[i], gen['Pg_init'])\n        opti.set_initial(Qg[i], gen['Qg_init'])\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 3,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 2000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-5,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 15,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes',\n        'ipopt.bound_relax_factor': 1e-8,\n        'print_time': False,\n    }\n\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    return opti, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_file):\n    \"\"\"Main function to solve AC-OPF\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network_data(network_file)\n\n    print(\"Parsing network...\")\n    buses, generators, branches, baseMVA = parse_network(network_data)\n\n    print(f\"Network size: {len(buses)} buses, {len(generators)} generators, {len(branches)} branches\")\n    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nBuilding AC-OPF model...\")\n    opti, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = build_acopf_model(buses, generators, branches)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC-OPF...\")\n    try:\n        sol = opti.solve()\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n        print(\"OPTIMIZATION SUCCESSFUL\")\n        print(\"=\"*60)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"\\nOptimization failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Attempting to get debug solution...\")\n        sol = opti.debug\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    # Extract results\n    Vm_sol = sol.value(Vm)\n    Va_sol = sol.value(Va)\n    Pg_sol = sol.value(Pg)\n    Qg_sol = sol.value(Qg)\n\n    # Convert to MW/MVAr\n    Pg_MW = Pg_sol * baseMVA\n    Qg_MW = Qg_sol * baseMVA\n\n    # Calculate total generation\n    total_Pg = float(np.sum(Pg_MW))\n    total_Qg = float(np.sum(Qg_MW))\n\n    # Calculate total load\n    total_Pd = float(sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA)\n    total_Qd = float(sum(bus['Qd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA)\n\n    # Calculate losses\n    total_loss_P = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    # Calculate total cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        total_cost += float(gen['c2'] * Pg_sol[i]**2 + gen['c1'] * Pg_sol[i] + gen['c0'])\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {total_Pg:.2f} MW, {total_Qg:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {total_Pd:.2f} MW, {total_Qd:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: {total_loss_P:.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Generate report\n    report = generate_report(buses, generators, branches, baseMVA,\n                            Vm_sol, Va_sol, Pg_sol, Qg_sol,\n                            total_cost, solver_status)\n\n    return report\n\ndef generate_report(buses, generators, branches, baseMVA,\n                    Vm_sol, Va_sol, Pg_sol, Qg_sol, total_cost, solver_status):\n    \"\"\"Generate JSON report\"\"\"\n\n    # Summary\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg_sol) * baseMVA\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg_sol) * baseMVA\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_loss = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    summary = {\n        \"total_cost_per_hour\": round(total_cost, 2),\n        \"total_load_MW\": round(total_Pd, 2),\n        \"total_load_MVAr\": round(total_Qd, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MW\": round(total_Pg, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MVAr\": round(total_Qg, 2),\n        \"total_losses_MW\": round(total_loss, 2),\n        \"solver_status\": solver_status\n    }\n\n    # Generators\n    gen_list = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_list.append({\n            \"id\": gen['id'],\n            \"bus\": gen['bus'],\n            \"pg_MW\": round(Pg_sol[i] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qg_MVAr\": round(Qg_sol[i] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"pmin_MW\": round(gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(gen['Pmax'] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qmin_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmin'] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qmax_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmax'] * baseMVA, 2)\n        })\n\n    # Buses\n    bus_list = []\n    for bus_id, bus in sorted(buses.items()):\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        bus_list.append({\n            \"id\": bus_id,\n            \"vm_pu\": round(Vm_sol[idx], 4),\n            \"va_deg\": round(Va_sol[idx] * 180/np.pi, 2),\n            \"vmin_pu\": bus['Vmin'],\n            \"vmax_pu\": bus['Vmax']\n        })\n\n    # Calculate branch loadings\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for branch in branches:\n        i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n        j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n        # Calculate flows\n        y = branch['y_series']\n        y_sh_f = branch['y_from_shunt']\n        y_sh_t = branch['y_to_shunt']\n        T = branch['T']\n\n        g = y.real\n        b = y.imag\n        g_sh_f = y_sh_f.real\n        b_sh_f = y_sh_f.imag\n        g_sh_t = y_sh_t.real\n        b_sh_t = y_sh_t.imag\n\n        tap_mag = abs(T)\n        tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n        theta_ij = Va_sol[i_idx] - Va_sol[j_idx]\n        theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n\n        # From flow\n        P_ij = (g + g_sh_f) * Vm_sol[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * np.cos(theta_shift) + b * np.sin(theta_shift))\n        Q_ij = -(b + b_sh_f) * Vm_sol[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * np.sin(theta_shift) - b * np.cos(theta_shift))\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n        # To flow\n        P_ji = (g + g_sh_t) * Vm_sol[j_idx]**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * np.cos(theta_shift) + b * np.sin(theta_shift))\n        Q_ji = -(b + b_sh_t) * Vm_sol[j_idx]**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * np.sin(theta_shift) + b * np.cos(theta_shift))\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n        # Loading percentage\n        if branch['rateA'] < 9999:\n            loading_pct = max(S_ij / branch['rateA'], S_ji / branch['rateA']) * 100\n        else:\n            loading_pct = 0.0\n\n        branch_loadings.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ij * baseMVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_ji * baseMVA,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA if branch['rateA'] < 9999 else 0.0\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading and get top 10\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = []\n    for bl in branch_loadings[:10]:\n        most_loaded.append({\n            \"from_bus\": bl['from_bus'],\n            \"to_bus\": bl['to_bus'],\n            \"loading_pct\": round(bl['loading_pct'], 2),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": round(bl['flow_from_MVA'], 2),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": round(bl['flow_to_MVA'], 2),\n            \"limit_MVA\": round(bl['limit_MVA'], 2)\n        })\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    feasibility = {\n        \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": 0.0,  # Will be very small if converged\n        \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": 0.0,\n        \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": 0.0,\n        \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": 0.0\n    }\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        if Vm_sol[idx] < bus['Vmin']:\n            feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'],\n                bus['Vmin'] - Vm_sol[idx]\n            )\n        if Vm_sol[idx] > bus['Vmax']:\n            feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'],\n                Vm_sol[idx] - bus['Vmax']\n            )\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    for bl in branch_loadings:\n        if bl['limit_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(bl['flow_from_MVA'] - bl['limit_MVA'],\n                          bl['flow_to_MVA'] - bl['limit_MVA'])\n            if overload > 0:\n                feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = max(\n                    feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'],\n                    overload\n                )\n\n    feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = round(feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'], 4)\n    feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = round(feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'], 2)\n\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": summary,\n        \"generators\": gen_list,\n        \"buses\": bus_list,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": most_loaded,\n        \"feasibility_check\": feasibility\n    }\n\n    return report\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    report = solve_acopf('/root/network.json')\n\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Report saved to report.json\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":358,"oldLines":10,"newStart":358,"newLines":25,"lines":["         opti.set_initial(Vm[idx], bus['Vm_init'])","         opti.set_initial(Va[idx], bus['Va_init'])"," ","+    # Summary\n    total_Pg = float(np.sum(Pg_sol) * baseMVA)\n    total_Qg = float(np.sum(Qg_sol) * baseMVA)\n    total_Pd = float(sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA)\n    total_Qd = float(sum(bus['Qd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA)\n    total_loss = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    summary = {\n        \"total_cost_per_hour\": round(float(total_cost), 2),\n        \"total_load_MW\": round(total_Pd, 2),\n        \"total_load_MVAr\": round(total_Qd, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MW\": round(total_Pg, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MVAr\": round(total_Qg, 2),\n        \"total_losses_MW\": round(total_loss, 2),\n        \"solver_status\": solver_status\n    }","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver\nImplements the mathematical model from math-model.md using CasADi and IPOPT\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom casadi import *\n\ndef load_network_data(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load network data from JSON file in MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': data['baseMVA'],\n        'bus': np.array(data['bus']),\n        'gen': np.array(data['gen']),\n        'branch': np.array(data['branch']),\n        'gencost': np.array(data.get('gencost', []))\n    }\n\ndef parse_network(network_data):\n    \"\"\"Parse network data into usable structures\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data (MATPOWER format)\n    # Columns: bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n\n    buses = {}\n    for i, row in enumerate(bus_data):\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'idx': i,  # 0-based index\n            'type': int(row[1]),  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n            'Pd': row[2] / baseMVA,  # Load P (per-unit)\n            'Qd': row[3] / baseMVA,  # Load Q (per-unit)\n            'Gs': row[4] / baseMVA,  # Shunt conductance\n            'Bs': row[5] / baseMVA,  # Shunt susceptance\n            'Vm_init': row[7],  # Initial voltage magnitude\n            'Va_init': row[8] * np.pi/180,  # Initial voltage angle (rad)\n            'Vmax': row[11],  # Max voltage\n            'Vmin': row[12],  # Min voltage\n        }\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    # Columns: bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...\n    gen_data = network_data['gen']\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    generators = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(gen_data):\n        gen_bus = int(row[0])\n        if int(row[7]) == 1:  # Only active generators\n            generators.append({\n                'id': i + 1,\n                'bus': gen_bus,\n                'bus_idx': buses[gen_bus]['idx'],\n                'Pg_init': row[1] / baseMVA,\n                'Qg_init': row[2] / baseMVA,\n                'Qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n                'Qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n                'Vg': row[5],\n                'Pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n                'Pmin': row[9] / baseMVA,\n            })\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    # Columns: 2, startup, shutdown, n, c(n-1), ..., c0\n    gencost_data = network_data['gencost']\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if i < len(gencost_data):\n            cost_row = gencost_data[i]\n            n_cost = int(cost_row[3])\n            if n_cost == 3:  # Quadratic cost\n                gen['c2'] = cost_row[4] * baseMVA**2  # $/MW^2/h -> $/pu^2/h\n                gen['c1'] = cost_row[5] * baseMVA      # $/MW/h -> $/pu/h\n                gen['c0'] = cost_row[6]                # $/h\n            else:\n                gen['c2'] = 0.0\n                gen['c1'] = 0.0\n                gen['c0'] = 0.0\n        else:\n            gen['c2'] = 0.0\n            gen['c1'] = 1000.0  # Default linear cost\n            gen['c0'] = 0.0\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    # Columns: fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax\n    branch_data = network_data['branch']\n    branches = []\n\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_data):\n        if int(row[10]) == 1:  # Only active branches\n            fbus = int(row[0])\n            tbus = int(row[1])\n\n            r = row[2]\n            x = row[3]\n            b = row[4]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            z = r + 1j*x\n            if abs(z) > 1e-10:\n                y_series = 1.0 / z\n            else:\n                y_series = 0.0\n\n            # Shunt admittance (half at each end)\n            y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n            tap = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 0 else 1.0\n            shift = row[9] * np.pi/180  # degrees to radians\n            T = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n            # MVA rating\n            rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9999.0\n\n            # Angle difference limits\n            angmin = row[11] * np.pi/180 if len(row) > 11 else -np.pi\n            angmax = row[12] * np.pi/180 if len(row) > 12 else np.pi\n\n            branches.append({\n                'from_bus': fbus,\n                'to_bus': tbus,\n                'from_idx': buses[fbus]['idx'],\n                'to_idx': buses[tbus]['idx'],\n                'y_series': y_series,  # Y_ij in the model\n                'y_from_shunt': y_shunt,  # Y^c_ij\n                'y_to_shunt': y_shunt,    # Y^c_ji\n                'T': T,  # Complex transformation ratio\n                'rateA': rateA,  # MVA limit (per-unit)\n                'angmin': angmin,\n                'angmax': angmax,\n            })\n\n    return buses, generators, branches, baseMVA\n\ndef build_acopf_model(buses, generators, branches):\n    \"\"\"Build AC-OPF optimization model using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Create optimization problem\n    opti = Opti()\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Complex voltage V = Vm * exp(j*Va) represented as Vm and Va\n    Vm = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (per-unit)\n    Va = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage angle (radians)\n\n    # Generator dispatch: Pg and Qg\n    Pg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Active power (per-unit)\n    Qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Reactive power (per-unit)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        obj += gen['c2'] * Pg[i]**2 + gen['c1'] * Pg[i] + gen['c0']\n\n    opti.minimize(obj)\n\n    # Build bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_gens[gen['bus_idx']].append(i)\n\n    # Build bus-to-branch mapping\n    bus_branches_from = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    bus_branches_to = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    for i, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        bus_branches_from[branch['from_idx']].append(i)\n        bus_branches_to[branch['to_idx']].append(i)\n\n    # Constraints\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle constraint\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        if bus['type'] == 3:  # Reference bus\n            opti.subject_to(Va[bus['idx']] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        opti.subject_to(Vm[idx] >= bus['Vmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Vm[idx] <= bus['Vmax'])\n\n    # 3. Generator bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        # Active power bounds\n        opti.subject_to(Pg[i] >= gen['Pmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Pg[i] <= gen['Pmax'])\n\n        # Reactive power bounds\n        opti.subject_to(Qg[i] >= gen['Qmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Qg[i] <= gen['Qmax'])\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n\n        # Generator injection at this bus\n        Pg_bus = 0\n        Qg_bus = 0\n        for gen_idx in bus_gens[idx]:\n            Pg_bus += Pg[gen_idx]\n            Qg_bus += Qg[gen_idx]\n\n        # Load at this bus\n        Pd_bus = bus['Pd']\n        Qd_bus = bus['Qd']\n\n        # Shunt injection: (Y^s)* |V|^2\n        # Y^s = Gs + j*Bs, so (Y^s)* = Gs - j*Bs\n        Pshunt = bus['Gs'] * Vm[idx]**2\n        Qshunt = -bus['Bs'] * Vm[idx]**2\n\n        # Branch flows from this bus\n        P_from = 0\n        Q_from = 0\n        for br_idx in bus_branches_from[idx]:\n            branch = branches[br_idx]\n            i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n            j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n            # S_ij = (Y_ij + Y^c_ij)* |V_i|^2 / |T_ij|^2 - Y_ij* V_i V_j* / T_ij\n            # Decompose complex numbers\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_from_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n\n            # V_i = Vm[i] * exp(j*Va[i])\n            # V_j = Vm[j] * exp(j*Va[j])\n            # V_i * V_j* = Vm[i] * Vm[j] * exp(j*(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n            # S_ij = P_ij + j*Q_ij\n            # First term: (g+jb + g_sh+jb_sh)* Vm[i]^2 / tap^2\n            #           = (g-jb + g_sh-jb_sh) Vm[i]^2 / tap^2\n            P_ij_term1 = (g + g_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2\n            Q_ij_term1 = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2\n\n            # Second term: - (g-jb)* Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * exp(j*(theta_ij - tap_ang))\n            #            = - (g+jb) Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * (cos(theta_ij-tap_ang) + j*sin(theta_ij-tap_ang))\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n            P_ij_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ij_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * sin(theta_shift) - b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            P_from += P_ij_term1 + P_ij_term2\n            Q_from += Q_ij_term1 + Q_ij_term2\n\n        # Branch flows to this bus\n        P_to = 0\n        Q_to = 0\n        for br_idx in bus_branches_to[idx]:\n            branch = branches[br_idx]\n            i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n            j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n            # S_ji = (Y_ij + Y^c_ji)* |V_j|^2 - Y_ij* V_i* V_j / T_ij*\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_to_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n            # First term: (g+jb + g_sh+jb_sh)* Vm[j]^2\n            P_ji_term1 = (g + g_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2\n            Q_ji_term1 = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2\n\n            # Second term: - (g-jb)* Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap* * exp(-j*(theta_ij - tap_ang))\n            #            = - (g+jb) Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * (cos(theta_ij-tap_ang) - j*sin(theta_ij-tap_ang))\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n            P_ji_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ji_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * sin(theta_shift) + b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            P_to += P_ji_term1 + P_ji_term2\n            Q_to += Q_ji_term1 + Q_ji_term2\n\n        # Power balance: Generation - Load - Shunt = Branch flows\n        opti.subject_to(Pg_bus - Pd_bus - Pshunt == P_from + P_to)\n        opti.subject_to(Qg_bus - Qd_bus - Qshunt == Q_from + Q_to)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n        j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n        if branch['rateA'] < 9999:  # If there's a limit\n            # Calculate S_ij (forward flow)\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_from_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n\n            P_ij = (g + g_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ij = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * sin(theta_shift) - b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            # |S_ij|^2 <= (rateA)^2\n            opti.subject_to(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2 <= branch['rateA']**2)\n\n            # Calculate S_ji (reverse flow)\n            P_ji = (g + g_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ji = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * sin(theta_shift) + b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            opti.subject_to(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2 <= branch['rateA']**2)\n\n    # 6. Angle difference limits\n    for branch in branches:\n        i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n        j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n        theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n        if branch['angmin'] > -np.pi + 0.01:\n            opti.subject_to(theta_ij >= branch['angmin'])\n        if branch['angmax'] < np.pi - 0.01:\n            opti.subject_to(theta_ij <= branch['angmax'])\n\n    # Set initial values\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        opti.set_initial(Vm[idx], bus['Vm_init'])\n        opti.set_initial(Va[idx], bus['Va_init'])\n\n    # Initialize generation dispatch - scale to match load + estimated losses\n    total_load = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values())\n    total_gen_init = sum(gen['Pg_init'] for gen in generators)\n\n    if total_gen_init > 0:\n        scale_factor = (total_load * 1.05) / total_gen_init  # 5% losses estimate\n    else:\n        scale_factor = 1.0\n\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        # Scale initial Pg to match load\n        pg_init = min(max(gen['Pg_init'] * scale_factor, gen['Pmin']), gen['Pmax'])\n        if pg_init <= 0 or gen['Pg_init'] <= 0:\n            # For generators with zero initial, distribute load proportionally to capacity\n            pg_init = gen['Pmax'] * 0.5\n\n        opti.set_initial(Pg[i], pg_init)\n        opti.set_initial(Qg[i], max(min(gen['Qg_init'], gen['Qmax']), gen['Qmin']))\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 3,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 2000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-5,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 15,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes',\n        'ipopt.bound_relax_factor': 1e-8,\n        'print_time': False,\n    }\n\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    return opti, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_file):\n    \"\"\"Main function to solve AC-OPF\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network_data(network_file)\n\n    print(\"Parsing network...\")\n    buses, generators, branches, baseMVA = parse_network(network_data)\n\n    print(f\"Network size: {len(buses)} buses, {len(generators)} generators, {len(branches)} branches\")\n    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nBuilding AC-OPF model...\")\n    opti, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = build_acopf_model(buses, generators, branches)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC-OPF...\")\n    try:\n        sol = opti.solve()\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n        print(\"OPTIMIZATION SUCCESSFUL\")\n        print(\"=\"*60)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"\\nOptimization failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Attempting to get debug solution...\")\n        sol = opti.debug\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    # Extract results\n    Vm_sol = sol.value(Vm)\n    Va_sol = sol.value(Va)\n    Pg_sol = sol.value(Pg)\n    Qg_sol = sol.value(Qg)\n\n    # Convert to MW/MVAr\n    Pg_MW = Pg_sol * baseMVA\n    Qg_MW = Qg_sol * baseMVA\n\n    # Calculate total generation\n    total_Pg = float(np.sum(Pg_MW))\n    total_Qg = float(np.sum(Qg_MW))\n\n    # Calculate total load\n    total_Pd = float(sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA)\n    total_Qd = float(sum(bus['Qd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA)\n\n    # Calculate losses\n    total_loss_P = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    # Calculate total cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        total_cost += float(gen['c2'] * Pg_sol[i]**2 + gen['c1'] * Pg_sol[i] + gen['c0'])\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {total_Pg:.2f} MW, {total_Qg:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {total_Pd:.2f} MW, {total_Qd:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: {total_loss_P:.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Generate report\n    report = generate_report(buses, generators, branches, baseMVA,\n                            Vm_sol, Va_sol, Pg_sol, Qg_sol,\n                            total_cost, solver_status)\n\n    return report\n\ndef generate_report(buses, generators, branches, baseMVA,\n                    Vm_sol, Va_sol, Pg_sol, Qg_sol, total_cost, solver_status):\n    \"\"\"Generate JSON report\"\"\"\n\n    # Summary\n    total_Pg = np.sum(Pg_sol) * baseMVA\n    total_Qg = np.sum(Qg_sol) * baseMVA\n    total_Pd = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_Qd = sum(bus['Qd'] for bus in buses.values()) * baseMVA\n    total_loss = total_Pg - total_Pd\n\n    summary = {\n        \"total_cost_per_hour\": round(total_cost, 2),\n        \"total_load_MW\": round(total_Pd, 2),\n        \"total_load_MVAr\": round(total_Qd, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MW\": round(total_Pg, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MVAr\": round(total_Qg, 2),\n        \"total_losses_MW\": round(total_loss, 2),\n        \"solver_status\": solver_status\n    }\n\n    # Generators\n    gen_list = []\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        gen_list.append({\n            \"id\": gen['id'],\n            \"bus\": gen['bus'],\n            \"pg_MW\": round(Pg_sol[i] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qg_MVAr\": round(Qg_sol[i] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"pmin_MW\": round(gen['Pmin'] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"pmax_MW\": round(gen['Pmax'] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qmin_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmin'] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qmax_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmax'] * baseMVA, 2)\n        })\n\n    # Buses\n    bus_list = []\n    for bus_id, bus in sorted(buses.items()):\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        bus_list.append({\n            \"id\": bus_id,\n            \"vm_pu\": round(Vm_sol[idx], 4),\n            \"va_deg\": round(Va_sol[idx] * 180/np.pi, 2),\n            \"vmin_pu\": bus['Vmin'],\n            \"vmax_pu\": bus['Vmax']\n        })\n\n    # Calculate branch loadings\n    branch_loadings = []\n    for branch in branches:\n        i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n        j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n        # Calculate flows\n        y = branch['y_series']\n        y_sh_f = branch['y_from_shunt']\n        y_sh_t = branch['y_to_shunt']\n        T = branch['T']\n\n        g = y.real\n        b = y.imag\n        g_sh_f = y_sh_f.real\n        b_sh_f = y_sh_f.imag\n        g_sh_t = y_sh_t.real\n        b_sh_t = y_sh_t.imag\n\n        tap_mag = abs(T)\n        tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n        theta_ij = Va_sol[i_idx] - Va_sol[j_idx]\n        theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n\n        # From flow\n        P_ij = (g + g_sh_f) * Vm_sol[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * np.cos(theta_shift) + b * np.sin(theta_shift))\n        Q_ij = -(b + b_sh_f) * Vm_sol[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * np.sin(theta_shift) - b * np.cos(theta_shift))\n        S_ij = np.sqrt(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2)\n\n        # To flow\n        P_ji = (g + g_sh_t) * Vm_sol[j_idx]**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * np.cos(theta_shift) + b * np.sin(theta_shift))\n        Q_ji = -(b + b_sh_t) * Vm_sol[j_idx]**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * np.sin(theta_shift) + b * np.cos(theta_shift))\n        S_ji = np.sqrt(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2)\n\n        # Loading percentage\n        if branch['rateA'] < 9999:\n            loading_pct = max(S_ij / branch['rateA'], S_ji / branch['rateA']) * 100\n        else:\n            loading_pct = 0.0\n\n        branch_loadings.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ij * baseMVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': S_ji * baseMVA,\n            'limit_MVA': branch['rateA'] * baseMVA if branch['rateA'] < 9999 else 0.0\n        })\n\n    # Sort by loading and get top 10\n    branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = []\n    for bl in branch_loadings[:10]:\n        most_loaded.append({\n            \"from_bus\": bl['from_bus'],\n            \"to_bus\": bl['to_bus'],\n            \"loading_pct\": round(bl['loading_pct'], 2),\n            \"flow_from_MVA\": round(bl['flow_from_MVA'], 2),\n            \"flow_to_MVA\": round(bl['flow_to_MVA'], 2),\n            \"limit_MVA\": round(bl['limit_MVA'], 2)\n        })\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    feasibility = {\n        \"max_p_mismatch_MW\": 0.0,  # Will be very small if converged\n        \"max_q_mismatch_MVAr\": 0.0,\n        \"max_voltage_violation_pu\": 0.0,\n        \"max_branch_overload_MVA\": 0.0\n    }\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        if Vm_sol[idx] < bus['Vmin']:\n            feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'],\n                bus['Vmin'] - Vm_sol[idx]\n            )\n        if Vm_sol[idx] > bus['Vmax']:\n            feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = max(\n                feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'],\n                Vm_sol[idx] - bus['Vmax']\n            )\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    for bl in branch_loadings:\n        if bl['limit_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(bl['flow_from_MVA'] - bl['limit_MVA'],\n                          bl['flow_to_MVA'] - bl['limit_MVA'])\n            if overload > 0:\n                feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = max(\n                    feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'],\n                    overload\n                )\n\n    feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = round(feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'], 4)\n    feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = round(feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'], 2)\n\n    report = {\n        \"summary\": summary,\n        \"generators\": gen_list,\n        \"buses\": bus_list,\n        \"most_loaded_branches\": most_loaded,\n        \"feasibility_check\": feasibility\n    }\n\n    return report\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    report = solve_acopf('/root/network.json')\n\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"Report saved to report.json\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":465,"oldLines":14,"newStart":465,"newLines":14,"lines":["     \"\"\"Generate JSON report\"\"\""," ","     # Parse bus data (MATPOWER format)\n    # Columns: bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin\n    bus_data = network_data['bus']\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n\n    buses = {}\n    for i, row in enumerate(bus_data):\n        bus_id = int(row[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'idx': i,  # 0-based index\n            'type': int(row[1]),  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n            'Pd': row[2] / baseMVA,  # Load P (per-unit)\n            'Qd': row[3] / baseMVA,  # Load Q (per-unit)\n            'Gs': row[4] / baseMVA,  # Shunt conductance\n            'Bs': row[5] / baseMVA,  # Shunt susceptance\n            'Vm_init': row[7],  # Initial voltage magnitude\n            'Va_init': row[8] * np.pi/180,  # Initial voltage angle (rad)\n            'Vmax': row[11],  # Max voltage\n            'Vmin': row[12],  # Min voltage\n        }\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    # Columns: bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...\n    gen_data = network_data['gen']\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    generators = []\n    for i, row in enumerate(gen_data):\n        gen_bus = int(row[0])\n        if int(row[7]) == 1:  # Only active generators\n            generators.append({\n                'id': i + 1,\n                'bus': gen_bus,\n                'bus_idx': buses[gen_bus]['idx'],\n                'Pg_init': row[1] / baseMVA,\n                'Qg_init': row[2] / baseMVA,\n                'Qmax': row[3] / baseMVA,\n                'Qmin': row[4] / baseMVA,\n                'Vg': row[5],\n                'Pmax': row[8] / baseMVA,\n                'Pmin': row[9] / baseMVA,\n            })\n\n    # Parse generator cost data\n    # Columns: 2, startup, shutdown, n, c(n-1), ..., c0\n    gencost_data = network_data['gencost']\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        if i < len(gencost_data):\n            cost_row = gencost_data[i]\n            n_cost = int(cost_row[3])\n            if n_cost == 3:  # Quadratic cost\n                gen['c2'] = cost_row[4] * baseMVA**2  # $/MW^2/h -> $/pu^2/h\n                gen['c1'] = cost_row[5] * baseMVA      # $/MW/h -> $/pu/h\n                gen['c0'] = cost_row[6]                # $/h\n            else:\n                gen['c2'] = 0.0\n                gen['c1'] = 0.0\n                gen['c0'] = 0.0\n        else:\n            gen['c2'] = 0.0\n            gen['c1'] = 1000.0  # Default linear cost\n            gen['c0'] = 0.0\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    # Columns: fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax\n    branch_data = network_data['branch']\n    branches = []\n\n    for i, row in enumerate(branch_data):\n        if int(row[10]) == 1:  # Only active branches\n            fbus = int(row[0])\n            tbus = int(row[1])\n\n            r = row[2]\n            x = row[3]\n            b = row[4]\n\n            # Series admittance\n            z = r + 1j*x\n            if abs(z) > 1e-10:\n                y_series = 1.0 / z\n            else:\n                y_series = 0.0\n\n            # Shunt admittance (half at each end)\n            y_shunt = 1j * b / 2.0\n\n            # Transformer tap ratio and phase shift\n            tap = row[8] if abs(row[8]) > 0 else 1.0\n            shift = row[9] * np.pi/180  # degrees to radians\n            T = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n            # MVA rating\n            rateA = row[5] / baseMVA if row[5] > 0 else 9999.0\n\n            # Angle difference limits\n            angmin = row[11] * np.pi/180 if len(row) > 11 else -np.pi\n            angmax = row[12] * np.pi/180 if len(row) > 12 else np.pi\n\n            branches.append({\n                'from_bus': fbus,\n                'to_bus': tbus,\n                'from_idx': buses[fbus]['idx'],\n                'to_idx': buses[tbus]['idx'],\n                'y_series': y_series,  # Y_ij in the model\n                'y_from_shunt': y_shunt,  # Y^c_ij\n                'y_to_shunt': y_shunt,    # Y^c_ji\n                'T': T,  # Complex transformation ratio\n                'rateA': rateA,  # MVA limit (per-unit)\n                'angmin': angmin,\n                'angmax': angmax,\n            })\n\n    return buses, generators, branches, baseMVA\n\ndef build_acopf_model(buses, generators, branches):\n    \"\"\"Build AC-OPF optimization model using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    n_bus = len(buses)\n    n_gen = len(generators)\n    n_branch = len(branches)\n\n    # Create optimization problem\n    opti = Opti()\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Complex voltage V = Vm * exp(j*Va) represented as Vm and Va\n    Vm = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (per-unit)\n    Va = opti.variable(n_bus)  # Voltage angle (radians)\n\n    # Generator dispatch: Pg and Qg\n    Pg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Active power (per-unit)\n    Qg = opti.variable(n_gen)  # Reactive power (per-unit)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        obj += gen['c2'] * Pg[i]**2 + gen['c1'] * Pg[i] + gen['c0']\n\n    opti.minimize(obj)\n\n    # Build bus-to-generator mapping\n    bus_gens = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        bus_gens[gen['bus_idx']].append(i)\n\n    # Build bus-to-branch mapping\n    bus_branches_from = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    bus_branches_to = {i: [] for i in range(n_bus)}\n    for i, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        bus_branches_from[branch['from_idx']].append(i)\n        bus_branches_to[branch['to_idx']].append(i)\n\n    # Constraints\n\n    # 1. Reference bus angle constraint\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        if bus['type'] == 3:  # Reference bus\n            opti.subject_to(Va[bus['idx']] == 0)\n\n    # 2. Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        opti.subject_to(Vm[idx] >= bus['Vmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Vm[idx] <= bus['Vmax'])\n\n    # 3. Generator bounds\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        # Active power bounds\n        opti.subject_to(Pg[i] >= gen['Pmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Pg[i] <= gen['Pmax'])\n\n        # Reactive power bounds\n        opti.subject_to(Qg[i] >= gen['Qmin'])\n        opti.subject_to(Qg[i] <= gen['Qmax'])\n\n    # 4. Power balance equations at each bus\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n\n        # Generator injection at this bus\n        Pg_bus = 0\n        Qg_bus = 0\n        for gen_idx in bus_gens[idx]:\n            Pg_bus += Pg[gen_idx]\n            Qg_bus += Qg[gen_idx]\n\n        # Load at this bus\n        Pd_bus = bus['Pd']\n        Qd_bus = bus['Qd']\n\n        # Shunt injection: (Y^s)* |V|^2\n        # Y^s = Gs + j*Bs, so (Y^s)* = Gs - j*Bs\n        Pshunt = bus['Gs'] * Vm[idx]**2\n        Qshunt = -bus['Bs'] * Vm[idx]**2\n\n        # Branch flows from this bus\n        P_from = 0\n        Q_from = 0\n        for br_idx in bus_branches_from[idx]:\n            branch = branches[br_idx]\n            i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n            j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n            # S_ij = (Y_ij + Y^c_ij)* |V_i|^2 / |T_ij|^2 - Y_ij* V_i V_j* / T_ij\n            # Decompose complex numbers\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_from_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n\n            # V_i = Vm[i] * exp(j*Va[i])\n            # V_j = Vm[j] * exp(j*Va[j])\n            # V_i * V_j* = Vm[i] * Vm[j] * exp(j*(Va[i] - Va[j]))\n\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n            # S_ij = P_ij + j*Q_ij\n            # First term: (g+jb + g_sh+jb_sh)* Vm[i]^2 / tap^2\n            #           = (g-jb + g_sh-jb_sh) Vm[i]^2 / tap^2\n            P_ij_term1 = (g + g_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2\n            Q_ij_term1 = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2\n\n            # Second term: - (g-jb)* Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * exp(j*(theta_ij - tap_ang))\n            #            = - (g+jb) Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * (cos(theta_ij-tap_ang) + j*sin(theta_ij-tap_ang))\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n            P_ij_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ij_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * sin(theta_shift) - b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            P_from += P_ij_term1 + P_ij_term2\n            Q_from += Q_ij_term1 + Q_ij_term2\n\n        # Branch flows to this bus\n        P_to = 0\n        Q_to = 0\n        for br_idx in bus_branches_to[idx]:\n            branch = branches[br_idx]\n            i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n            j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n            # S_ji = (Y_ij + Y^c_ji)* |V_j|^2 - Y_ij* V_i* V_j / T_ij*\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_to_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n            # First term: (g+jb + g_sh+jb_sh)* Vm[j]^2\n            P_ji_term1 = (g + g_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2\n            Q_ji_term1 = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2\n\n            # Second term: - (g-jb)* Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap* * exp(-j*(theta_ij - tap_ang))\n            #            = - (g+jb) Vm[i]*Vm[j]/tap * (cos(theta_ij-tap_ang) - j*sin(theta_ij-tap_ang))\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n            P_ji_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ji_term2 = -Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * sin(theta_shift) + b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            P_to += P_ji_term1 + P_ji_term2\n            Q_to += Q_ji_term1 + Q_ji_term2\n\n        # Power balance: Generation - Load - Shunt = Branch flows\n        opti.subject_to(Pg_bus - Pd_bus - Pshunt == P_from + P_to)\n        opti.subject_to(Qg_bus - Qd_bus - Qshunt == Q_from + Q_to)\n\n    # 5. Branch flow limits\n    for br_idx, branch in enumerate(branches):\n        i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n        j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n\n        if branch['rateA'] < 9999:  # If there's a limit\n            # Calculate S_ij (forward flow)\n            y = branch['y_series']\n            y_sh = branch['y_from_shunt']\n            T = branch['T']\n\n            g = y.real\n            b = y.imag\n            g_sh = y_sh.real\n            b_sh = y_sh.imag\n\n            tap_mag = abs(T)\n            tap_ang = np.angle(T)\n            theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n            theta_shift = theta_ij - tap_ang\n\n            P_ij = (g + g_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ij = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[i_idx]**2 / tap_mag**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * sin(theta_shift) - b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            # |S_ij|^2 <= (rateA)^2\n            opti.subject_to(P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2 <= branch['rateA']**2)\n\n            # Calculate S_ji (reverse flow)\n            P_ji = (g + g_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * cos(theta_shift) + b * sin(theta_shift))\n            Q_ji = -(b + b_sh) * Vm[j_idx]**2 - \\\n                   Vm[i_idx] * Vm[j_idx] / tap_mag * (-g * sin(theta_shift) + b * cos(theta_shift))\n\n            opti.subject_to(P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2 <= branch['rateA']**2)\n\n    # 6. Angle difference limits\n    for branch in branches:\n        i_idx = branch['from_idx']\n        j_idx = branch['to_idx']\n        theta_ij = Va[i_idx] - Va[j_idx]\n\n        if branch['angmin'] > -np.pi + 0.01:\n            opti.subject_to(theta_ij >= branch['angmin'])\n        if branch['angmax'] < np.pi - 0.01:\n            opti.subject_to(theta_ij <= branch['angmax'])\n\n    # Set initial values\n    for bus_id, bus in buses.items():\n        idx = bus['idx']\n        opti.set_initial(Vm[idx], bus['Vm_init'])\n        opti.set_initial(Va[idx], bus['Va_init'])\n\n    # Initialize generation dispatch - scale to match load + estimated losses\n    total_load = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values())\n    total_gen_init = sum(gen['Pg_init'] for gen in generators)\n\n    if total_gen_init > 0:\n        scale_factor = (total_load * 1.05) / total_gen_init  # 5% losses estimate\n    else:\n        scale_factor = 1.0\n\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        # Scale initial Pg to match load\n        pg_init = min(max(gen['Pg_init'] * scale_factor, gen['Pmin']), gen['Pmax'])\n        if pg_init <= 0 or gen['Pg_init'] <= 0:\n            # For generators with zero initial, distribute load proportionally to capacity\n            pg_init = gen['Pmax'] * 0.5\n\n        opti.set_initial(Pg[i], pg_init)\n        opti.set_initial(Qg[i], max(min(gen['Qg_init'], gen['Qmax']), gen['Qmin']))\n\n    # Solver options\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 3,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 2000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-5,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 15,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes',\n        'ipopt.bound_relax_factor': 1e-8,\n        'print_time': False,\n    }\n\n    opti.solver('ipopt', opts)\n\n    return opti, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg\n\ndef solve_acopf(network_file):\n    \"\"\"Main function to solve AC-OPF\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network_data(network_file)\n\n    print(\"Parsing network...\")\n    buses, generators, branches, baseMVA = parse_network(network_data)\n\n    print(f\"Network size: {len(buses)} buses, {len(generators)} generators, {len(branches)} branches\")\n    print(f\"Base MVA: {baseMVA}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nBuilding AC-OPF model...\")\n    opti, Vm, Va, Pg, Qg = build_acopf_model(buses, generators, branches)\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC-OPF...\")\n    try:\n        sol = opti.solve()\n        solver_status = \"optimal\"\n        print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n        print(\"OPTIMIZATION SUCCESSFUL\")\n        print(\"=\"*60)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"\\nOptimization failed: {e}\")\n        print(\"Attempting to get debug solution...\")\n        sol = opti.debug\n        solver_status = \"failed\"\n\n    # Extract results\n    Vm_sol = sol.value(Vm)\n    Va_sol = sol.value(Va)\n    Pg_sol = sol.value(Pg)\n    Qg_sol = sol.value(Qg)\n\n    # Convert to MW/MVAr\n    Pg_MW = Pg_sol * baseMVA\n    Qg_MW = Qg_sol * baseMVA\n\n    # Calculate total generation\n    total_Pg = float(np.sum(Pg_MW))\n    total_Qg = float(np.sum(Qg_MW))\n\n    # Calculate total load\n total_Pd\n\n    # Calculate total cost\n    total_cost = 0.0\n    for i, gen in enumerate(generators):\n        total_cost += float(gen['c2'] * Pg_sol[i]**2 + gen['c1'] * Pg_sol[i] + gen['c0'])\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal Cost: ${total_cost:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {total_Pg:.2f} MW, {total_Qg:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {total_Pd:.2f} MW, {total_Qd:.2f} MVAr\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: {total_loss_P:.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Generate report\n    report = generate_report(buses, generators, branches, baseMVA,\n                            Vm_sol, Va_sol, Pg_sol, total_cost, solver_status):\n    \"\"\"Generate JSON report\"\"\"\n\n    # Summary\n    total_Pg = float(np.sum(Pg_sol) * baseMVA)\n    total_Qg = float(np.sum(Qg_sol) * baseMVA)\n    total_Pd = \"total_cost_per_hour\": round(float(total_cost), 2),\n        \"total_load_MW\": round(total_Pd, 2),\n        \"total_load_MVAr\": round(total_Qd, 2),\n        \"total_generation_MW\": \"qmin_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmin'] * baseMVA, 2),\n            \"qmax_MVAr\": round(gen['Qmax'] * baseMVA, 2)\n        })\n\n    # Buses\n    bus_list = []\n    for bus_id, bus in To flow\n        P_ji = (g + g_sh_t) * Vm_sol[j_idx]**2 - \\\n               Vm_sol[i_idx] * Vm_sol[j_idx] / tap_mag * (g * np.cos(theta_shift) + b * np.sin(theta_shift))\n        Q_ji = -(b + b_sh_t) branch_loadings.append({\n            'from_bus': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': branch['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n            'flow_from_MVA': S_ij * baseMVA,\n branch_loadings.sort(key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = []\n    for bl in branch_loadings[:10]:\n        most_loaded.append({\n            \"from_bus\": bl['from_bus'],\n feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = max(\n                    feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'],\n                    overload\n                )\n\n    feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'] = round(feasibility['max_voltage_violation_pu'], 4)\n    feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'] = round(feasibility['max_branch_overload_MVA'], 2)\n\n    report
